  -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 -- 5 stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model\n//  (ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, CARRY_W=5, SUM_WIDTH=19, wrapped 6 bit\n//  exponent arithmetic, RNE rounding and NaR handling).\n//\n//  Pipeline plan (5 register stages, latency 5):\n//    A : posit decode (direct regime extraction) + exponent add + 4x4 mant mul\n//    B : 17-way maximum exponent tree\n//    C : alignment shift + sign folding (bit invert) + popcount\n//    D : balanced 17-way adder tree + sign correction\n//    E : normalisation (lzc + shift)\n//    F : posit encode\n//\n//  Cost saving ideas vs. the reference:\n//    * decode without a 15 bit lzc and a 15 bit barrel shifter\n//    * two's complement of the aligned words replaced by a bit inversion plus\n//      one constant (K - K<<14) added at the very end\n//    * balanced binary adder tree instead of a 17:2 carry-save tree plus a\n//      20 bit carry-propagate adder\n//    * encoder shifts a 24 bit window instead of a 46 bit one\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode  ->  {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n    //    rg_exp = 4*regime_k + exponent      (6 bit two's complement)\n    //    mant   = {implicit, frac[2:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [10:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x;\n        logic [2:0] hsb;\n        logic [3:0] rl, ek;\n        logic [6:0] al;\n        va  = p[7] ? (7'd0 - p[6:0]) : p[6:0];\n        x   = va[6] ? ~va : va;\n        hsb = 3'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 7; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[2:0];\n        rl  = 4'd6 - {1'b0, hsb};\n        al  = va << (4'd7 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        ek  = va[6] ? (rl - 4'd1) : (4'd0 - rl);\n        dec8 = {p[7], ek, al[6:5], |p[6:0], al[4:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(16,2) decode ->  {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[11:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [18:0] dec16(input [15:0] p);\n        logic [14:0] va, x;\n        logic [3:0]  hsb, rl;\n        logic [4:0]  ek;\n        logic [14:0] al;\n        va  = p[15] ? (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        x   = va[14] ? ~va : va;\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        rl  = 4'd14 - hsb;\n        al  = va << (4'd15 - hsb);\n        ek  = va[14] ? ({1'b0, rl} - 5'd1) : (5'd0 - {1'b0, rl});\n        dec16 = {p[15], ek[3:0], al[14:13], |p[14:0], al[12:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic [5:0] smax(input [5:0] a, input [5:0] b);\n        smax = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  STAGE A : decode, exponent add, mantissa multiply\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [16:0] a_sg;\n    logic [5:0]  a_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] a_wd [0:16];\n\n    generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) begin : g_dec\n        logic [10:0] da, db;\n        logic [7:0]  pr;\n        assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*8 +: 8]);\n        assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*8 +: 8]);\n        assign a_sg[i]  = da[10] ^ db[10];\n        assign a_ep[i]  = da[9:4] + db[9:4];\n        assign pr       = da[3:0] * db[3:0];\n        assign a_wd[i]  = {pr, 6'b0};\n    end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [18:0] dacc;\n    assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n    assign a_sg[16] = dacc[18];\n    assign a_ep[16] = dacc[17:12];\n    assign a_wd[16] = {1'b0, dacc[11:0], 1'b0};\n\n    logic [16:0] b_sg;\n    logic [5:0]  b_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] b_wd [0:16];\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        b_sg <= a_sg;\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin\n            b_ep[i] <= a_ep[i];\n            b_wd[i] <= a_wd[i];\n        end\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  STAGE B : 17-way maximum exponent\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [5:0] c_emax;\n    logic [16:0] c_sg;\n    logic [13:0] c_cw [0:16];\n\n    logic [5:0] mx1 [0:7];\n    logic [5:0] mx2 [0:3];\n    logic [5:0] mx3 [0:1];\n    logic [5:0] mx4, b_emax;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) pc1[i] = {4'b0, c_sg[2*i]} + {4'b0, c_sg[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) pc2[i] = {1'b0, pc1[2*i]} + {1'b0, pc1[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) pc3[i] = {1'b0, pc2[2*i]} + {1'b0, pc2[2*i+1]};\n        pc4   = pc3[0] + pc3[1] + {5'b0, c_sg[16]};\n        ksum  = pc4[4:0];\n\n        // sum of the complemented / aligned words\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) t1[i] = {1'b0, c_cw[2*i]} + {1'b0, c_cw[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) t2[i] = {1'b0, t1[2*i]} + {1'b0, t1[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) t3[i] = {1'b0, t2[2*i]} + {1'b0, t2[2*i+1]};\n        t4 = {1'b0, t3[0]} + {1'b0, t3[1]};\n        t5 = {1'b0, t4} + {5'b0, c_cw[16]};\n        // add  K - (K<<14)   (mod 2^20)  to complete the two's complement\n        t6 = {1'b0, t5} + {|ksum, (5'd0 - ksum), 9'b0, ksum};\n    end\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        d_tot  <= t6;\n        d_emax <= c_emax;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  STAGE D : normalisation\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic        e_sign;\n    logic [5:0]  e_rgexp;\n    logic [13:0] e_fmant;\n\n    logic [18:0] sm, sn;\n    logic [4:0]  lzc;\n    logic        empty;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        e_sign = d_tot[19];\n        sm     = e_sign ? (~d_tot[18:0] + 19'd1) : d_tot[18:0];\n        empty  = ~(|sm);\n        lzc    = 5'd19;\n        for (i = 0; i < 19; i++) if (sm[i]) lzc = 5'd18 - i[4:0];\n        sn     = sm << lzc;\n        e_rgexp = d_emax + (empty ? 6'sd0 : (6'sd6 - $signed({1'b0, lzc})));\n        e_fmant = {sn[18:6], |sn[5:0]};\n    end\n\n    logic        f_sign;\n    logic [5:0]  f_rgexp;\n    logic [13:0] f_fmant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        f_sign  <= e_sign;\n        f_rgexp <= e_rgexp;\n        f_fmant <= e_fmant;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  STAGE E : posit encode\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [3:0]  rb, sh;\n    logic [23:0] t24, y;\n    logic [14:0] vbr, var;\n    logic        stk, rnd, rval;\n    logic [15:0] enc;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        // regime field length:  k>=0 -> k+2 ; k<0 -> |k|+1   (always in 2..9)\n        rb  = f_rgexp[5] ? Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, CARRY_W=5, SUM_WIDTH=19, the wrapped exponent\n//  arithmetic of the reference decoder and the NaR handling.\n//\n//  stage 1 : posit decode (direct regime extraction) + exponent add + 4x4 mul\n//  stage 2 : 17-way maximum exponent tree\n//  stage 3 : alignment shift + sign folding (bit inversion) + popcount\n//  stage 4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction\n//  stage 5 : normalisation (lzc + shift) and posit encoding\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode -> {rg_exp[4:0], nz, frac[2:0]}\n    //    rg_exp = (4*regime_k + exp) mod 16          (5 bit unsigned)\n    //    frac   = first 3 fraction bits after the exponent field\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [8:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [2:0] hsb, rl, rb;\n        logic [3:0] ek;\n        logic       rs, emp;\n        va  = p[7] ? (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        rs  = va[14];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        emp = (x == 15'd0);\n        rl  = emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd14 - hsb);\n        rb  = emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd15 - hsb);\n        ek  = rs ? g < 16; g++) begin : gdec\n        assign d8a[g] = dec8(operands_a[g*8 +: 8]);\n        assign d8b[g] = dec8(operands_b[g*8 +: 8]);\n    end\n    endgenerate\n    assign d16 = dec16(acc);\n\n    logic [16:0] s_sg;\n    logic [4:0]  s_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] s_wd [0:16];\n    logic [4:0]  s_k;\n    logic [1:0]  pc1 [0:7];\n    logic [2:0]  pc2 [0:3];\n    logic [3:0]  pc3 [0:1];\n    logic [4:0]  pc4;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) pc1[i] = {1'b0, s_sg[2*i]} + {1'b0, s_sg[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) pc2[i] = {1'b0, pc1[2*i]} + {1'b0, pc1[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) pc3[i] = {1'b0, pc2[2*i]} + {1'b0, pc2[2*i+1]};\n        pc4 = {1'b0, pc3[0]} + {1'b0, pc3[1]};\n        s_k = pc4 + {4'b0, s_sg[16]};\n    end\n\n    logic [16:0] p1_sg;\n    logic [4:0]  p1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] p1_wd [0:16];\n    logic [4:0]  p1_k;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p1_sg <= s_sg;\n        p1_k  <= s_k;\n        for (i = 0; (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        rs  = va[14];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 15'd0);\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 5'd15 : (5'd14 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        rb  = emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd15 - hsb);\n        ek  = rs  ?   -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 : 5-stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, the SUM_WIDTH=19 wrap-around, the reference\n//  decoder/encoder conventions (regime_k in a narrow signed field, shift amount\n//  clamped at MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT) and the NaR handling.\n//\n//  stage 1 : posit decode + exponent add + 4x4 mantissa multiply\n//  stage 2 : 17-way signed maximum exponent tree\n//  stage 3 : alignment barrel shift + sign folding (bit inversion) + popcount\n//  stage 4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction\n//  stage 5 : normalisation and posit encoding\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  leading zero count helpers (lzc(0) == width)\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [2:0] lz7(input [6:0] x);\n        logic [1:0] a, b;\n        logic       h, g;\n        h = |x[6:4];\n        g = |x[2:0];\n        a = h ? (5'd1 - k5) : (k5 + 5'd2);\n        e_s1 = (e_rb > 5'd16) ? 5'd15 : (e_rb - 5'd1);\n        e_um = {sk ? 15'h0001 : 15'h7FFE, e_rex[1:0], e_fmant[12:0]};\n        e_q  = e_um >> e_s1;\n        e_vbr  = e_q[15:1];\n        e_rbit = e_q[0];\n        msk    = (15'd1 << e_s1) - 15'd1;\n        e_stk  = |(e_um[14:0] & msk);\n        e_rval = e_rbit & (e_stk | e_vbr[0]);\n        e_var  = e_vbr + {14'b0, e_rval};\n        e_res  = sk ? {1'b1, ~e_var + 15'd1} : {1'b0, e_var};\n    end\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        result_o <= e_fmant[13] ? e_res : 16'd0;\n    end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v3.sv')

  -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 : 5-stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, the 20-bit two's complement wrap-around, the\n//  reference decoder/encoder conventions and the NaR handling.\n//\n//  R1 : posit decode (direct regime extraction, no 15-bit lzc/shifter)\n//  R2 : exponent add, 4x4 mantissa multiply, 17-way max exponent\n//  R3 : alignment shift, sign folding (bit inversion), sign popcount\n//  R4 : carry-save reduction of the 18 aligned operands + final CPA\n//  R5 : normalisation (lzc + shift) and posit encoding\n//\n//  Two's complement of the aligned words is replaced by a bit inversion plus\n//  one constant (K - K<<14) added once in the reduction tree, so the 17\n//  20-bit adders of the reference disappear.\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode -> {rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [9:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [2:0] lz, rb;\n        logic [3:0] k;\n        logic       rs;\n        va = p[7] ?   -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 : 5-stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, the 20-bit two's complement wrap-around, the\n//  reference decoder/encoder conventions and the NaR handling.\n//\n//   R1 : posit decode + exponent add + 5x5 mantissa multiply\n//   R2 : 17-way signed maximum exponent tree\n//   R3 : alignment barrel shift + sign folding (bit inversion) + popcount\n//   R4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction constant\n//   R5 : normalisation and posit encoding\n//\n//  Optimisations vs. the reference:\n//   * decode without a 15-bit leading-zero counter and 15-bit barrel shifter\n//   * two's complement of the 17 aligned words replaced by a bit inversion\n//     plus one constant (K - K<<14) added once at the end\n//   * balanced binary adder tree instead of a 17:2 carry-save tree followed by\n//     a 20-bit carry-propagate adder\n//   * encoder shifts a 30-bit window by (16 - regime_length) instead of a\n//     46-bit window by the regime length\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode -> {rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [9:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x;\n        logic [2:0] hsb, rl, rb;\n        logic [3:0] k;\n        logic [6:0] al;\n        logic       rs, emp;\n        va  = p[7] ? (7'd0 - p[6:0]) : p[6:0];\n        rs  = va[6];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 7'd0);\n        hsb = 3'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 7; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[2:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 3'd7 : (3'd6 - hsb);\n        rb  = emp ? 3'd7 : (3'd7 - hsb);\n        k   = rs ? ({1'b0, rl} - 4'd1) : (4'd0 - {1'b0, rl});\n        al  = va << rb;\n        dec8 = {{2'b0, k}, al[6:5], |va, al[4:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(16,2) decode -> {rg_exp[6:0], mant[13:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [20:0] dec16(input [15:0] p);\n        logic [14:0] va, x;\n        logic [3:0]  hsb, rl, rb;\n        logic [4:0]  k;\n        logic [14:0] al;\n        logic        rs, emp;\n        va  = p[15] ? (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        rs  = va[14];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 15'd0);\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd14 - hsb);\n        rb  = emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd15 - hsb);\n        k   = rs ? (rl - 5'd1) : (5'd0 - rl);\n        al  = va << rb;\n        dec16 = {{2'b0, k}, al[14:13], |va, al[12:0]};\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic [6:0] smax(input [6:0] a, input [6:0] b);\n        smax = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R1 : decode, exponent add, mantissa multiply\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [16:0] s1_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  s1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] s1_wd [0:16];\n\n    genvar g;\n    generate\n    for (g = 0; g < 16; g++) begin : gdec\n        logic [9:0]  da, db;\n        logic [9:0]  pr;\n        assign da = dec8(operands_a[g*8 +: 8]);\n        assign db = dec8(operands_b[g*8 +: 8]);\n        assign s1_sg[g] = operands_a[g*8+7] ^ operands_b[g*8+7];\n        assign s1_ep[g] = {{2{da[9]}}, da[9:4]} + {{2{db[9]}}, db[9:4]};\n        assign pr       = da[3:0] * db[3:0];\n        assign s1_wd[g] = pr[7:0] * 8'd64;\n    end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [20:0] dacc;\n    assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n    assign s1_sg[16] = acc[15];\n    assign s1_ep[16] = dacc[20:14];\n    assign s1_wd[16] = dacc[13:0];\n\n    logic [16:0] p1_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  p1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] p1_wd [0:16];\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p1_sg <= s1_sg;\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin\n            p1_ep[i] <= s1_ep[i];\n            p1_wd[i] <= s1_wd[i];\n        end\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R2 : 17-way signed maximum exponent\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [6:0] s2_emax;\n    logic [6:0] lv1 [0:7];\n    logic [6:0] lv2 [0:3];\n    logic [6:0] lv3 [0:1];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) lv1[i] = smax(p1_ep[2*i], p1_ep[2*i+1]);\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) lv2[i] = smax(lv1[2*i], lv1[2*i+1]);\n        lv3[0] = smax(lv2[0], lv2[1]);\n        lv3[1] = smax(lv2[2], lv2[3]);\n        s2_emax = smax(smax(lv3[0], lv3[1]), p1_ep[16]);\n    end\n\n    logic [16:0] p2_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  p2_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] p2_wd [0:16];\n    logic [6:0]  p2_emax;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p2_sg   <= p1_sg;\n        p2_emax <= s2_emax;\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin\n            p2_ep[i] <= p1_ep[i];\n            p2_wd[i] <= p1_wd[i];\n        end\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R3 : alignment shift + sign folding + sign popcount\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [13:0] s3_cw [0:16];\n    logic [4:0]  s3_k;\n\n    generate\n    for (g = 0; g < 17; g++) begin : gal\n        logic [6:0] df;\n        logic [3:0] sh;\n        assign df = p2_emax - p2_ep[g];\n        assign sh = (|df[6:4]) ? 4'd14 : df[3:0];\n        assign s3_cw[g] = (p2_wd[g] >> sh) ^ {14{p2_sg[g]}};\n    end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [1:0] k1 [0:7];\n    logic [2:0] k2 [0:3];\n    logic [3:0] k3 [0:1];\n    logic [4:0] k4;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) k1[i] = {1'b0, p2_sg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p2_sg[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) k2[i] = {1'b0, k1[2*i]} + {1'b0, k1[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) k3[i] = {1'b0, k2[2*i]} + {1'b0, k2[2*i+1]};\n        k4   = {1'b0, k3[0]} + {1'b0, k3[1]};\n        s3_k = k4 + {4'b0, p2_sg[16]};\n    end\n\n    logic [13:0] p3_cw [0:16];\n    logic [4:0]  p3_k;\n    logic [6:0]  p3_emax;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p3_k    <= s3_k;\n        p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) p3_cw[i] <= s3_cw[i];\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [14:0] d1 [0:7];\n    logic [15:0] d2 [0:3];\n    logic [16:0] d3 [0:1];\n    logic [17:0] d4;\n    logic [18:0] d5;\n    logic [19:0] s4_sum;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) d1[i] = {1'b0, p3_cw[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_cw[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) d2[i] = {1'b0, d1[2*i]} + {1'b0, d1[2*i+1]};\n        d3[0] = {1'b0, d2[0]} + {1'b0, d2[1]};\n        d3[1] = {1'b0, d2[2]} + {1'b0, d2[3]};\n        d4    = {1'b0, d3[0]} + {1'b0, d3[1]};\n        d5    = {1'b0, d4} + {5'b0, p3_cw[16]};\n        s4_sum = {1'b0, d5} + {15'b0, p3_k} - {1'b0, p3_k, 14'b0};\n    end\n\n    logic [19:0] p4_sum;\n    logic [6:0]  p4_emax;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p4_sum  <= s4_sum;\n        p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R5 : normalisation + posit encoding\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic        e_sign;\n    logic [18:0] e_mag;\n    logic [4:0]  e_lzc;\n    logic [6:0]  e_rgexp;\n    logic [13:0] e_fmant;\n    logic        e_nz;\n\n    logic [4:0]  e_k5, e_rb, e_sh;\n    logic [4:0]  e_t;\n    logic [29:0] e_x;\n    logic [30:0] e_r;\n    logic [14:0] e_vbr, e_var;\n    logic        e_rbit, e_stk, e_rval;\n    logic [15:0] e_res;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        logic [18:0] sn;\n        logic [6:0]  adj;\n        logic        sk;\n\n        // ---- magnitude / leading zero count / normalisation ----\n        e_sign = p4_sum[19];\n        e_mag  = e_sign ? (~p4_sum[18:0] + 19'd1) : p4_sum[18:0];\n        e_nz   = |e_mag;\n        e_lzc  = 5'd19;\n        for (i = 0; i < 19; i++) if (e_mag[i]) e_lzc = 5'd18 - i[4:0];\n        adj    = e_nz ? (7'sd6 - $signed({2'b0, e_lzc})) : 7'sd0;\n        e_rgexp = $signed({1'b0, p4_emax}) + adj;\n        sn     = e_mag << e_lzc;\n        e_fmant = {sn[18], sn[17:6], |sn[5:0]};\n\n        // ---- posit encode ----\n        sk   = e_rgexp[6];\n        e_k5 = e_rgexp[6:2];\n        e_rb = sk ? (5'd1 - e_k5) : (e_k5 + 5'd2);\n        e_sh = (e_rb > 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : e_rb;\n        e_t  = 5'd16 - e_sh;\n        e_x  = {sk ? 15'h0001 : 15'h7FFE, e_rgexp[1:0], e_fmant[12:0]};\n        e_r  = {1'b0, e_x} << e_t;\n        e_vbr  = e_r[30:16];\n        e_rbit = e_r[15];\n        e_stk  = |e_r[14:0];\n        e_rval = e_rbit & (e_stk | e_vbr[0]);\n        e_var  = e_vbr + {14'b0, e_rval};\n        e_res  = sk ? {1'b1, ~e_var + 15'd1} : {1'b0, e_var};\n    end\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        result_o <= e_fmant[13] ? e_res : 16'd0;\n    end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='c1.sv')

  -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 : 5-stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, the 20-bit two's complement wrap-around, the\n//  reference decoder/encoder conventions and the NaR handling.\n//\n//   R1 : posit decode + exponent add + 5x5 mantissa multiply\n//   R2 : 17-way signed maximum exponent tree\n//   R3 : alignment barrel shift + sign folding (bit inversion) + popcount\n//   R4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction constant\n//   R5 : normalisation and posit encoding\n//\n//  Optimisations vs. the reference:\n//   * decode without a 15-bit leading-zero counter and 15-bit barrel shifter\n//   * two's complement of the 17 aligned words replaced by a bit inversion\n//     plus one constant (K - K<<14) added once at the end\n//   * balanced binary adder tree instead of a 17:2 carry-save tree followed by\n//     a 20-bit carry-propagate adder\n//   * encoder shifts a 30-bit window by the regime length instead of a 46-bit\n//     window by the complement of it\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode -> {rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n    //    rg_exp = (4*regime_k + exp)          (6-bit signed)\n    //    mant   = {implicit, frac[2:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [9:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [2:0] hsb;\n        logic [3:0] rl, k, rg;\n        logic       rs, emp;\n        va  = p[7] ? (7'd0 - p[6:0]) : p[6:0];\n        rs  = va[6];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 7'd0);\n        hsb = 3'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 7; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[2:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 4'd7 : (4'd6 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        k   = rs ? (rl - 4'd1) : (4'd0 - rl);\n        al  = va << (emp ? 3'd7 : (3'd7 - hsb));\n        rg  = ({{2{k[3]}}, k} << 2) | {4'b0, al[6:5]};\n        dec8 = {rg[5:0], |va, al[4:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(16,2) decode -> {rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [18:0] dec16(input [15:0] p);\n        logic [14:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [3:0]  hsb;\n        logic [4:0]  rl, k;\n        logic [6:0]  rg;\n        logic        rs, emp;\n        va  = p[15] ? (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        rs  = va[14];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 15'd0);\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 5'd15 : (5'd14 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        k   = rs ? (rl - 5'd1) : (5'd0 - rl);\n        al  = va << (emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd15 - hsb));\n        rg  = ({{2{k[4]}}, k} << 2) | {5'b0, al[14:13]};\n        dec16 = {rg, |va, al[12:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic [6:0] smax(input [6:0] a, input [6:0] b);\n        smax = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R1 : decode, exponent add, mantissa multiply\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [16:0] s1_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  s1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] s1_wd [0:16];\n\n    genvar g;\n    generate\n    for (g = 0; g < 16; g++) begin : gdec\n        logic [9:0] da, db;\n        logic [7:0] pr;\n        assign da = dec8(operands_a[g*8 +: 8]);\n        assign db = dec8(operands_b[g*8 +: 8]);\n        assign s1_sg[g] = operands_a[g*8+7] ^ operands_b[g*8+7];\n        assign s1_ep[g] = {{da[9]}, da[9:4]} + {{db[9]}, db[9:4]};\n        assign pr       = da[3:0] * db[3:0];\n        assign s1_wd[g] = {2'b0, pr, 4'b0};\n    end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [18:0] dacc;\n    assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n    assign s1_sg[16] = acc[15];\n    assign s1_ep[16] = dacc[18:12];\n    assign s1_wd[16] = {1'b0, dacc[11:0], 1'b0};\n\n    logic [16:0] p1_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  p1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] p1_wd [0:16];\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p1_sg <= s1_sg;\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin\n            p1_ep[i] <= s1_ep[i];\n            p1_wd[i] <= s1_wd[i];\n        end\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R2 : 17-way signed maximum exponent\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [6:0] s2_emax;\n    logic [6:0] lv1 [0:7];\n    logic [6:0] lv2 [0:3];\n    logic [6:0] lv3 [0:1];\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin\n            p2_ep[i] <= p1_ep[i];\n            p2_wd[i] <= p1_wd[i];\n        end\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R3 : alignment shift + sign folding + sign popcount\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [13:0] s3_cw [0:16];\n    logic [4:0]  s3_k;\n\n    generate\n    for (g = 0; i < 17; i++) p3_cw[i] <= s3_cw[i];\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [14:0] d1 [0:7];\n    logic [15:0] d2 [0:3];\n    logic [16:0] d3 [0:1];\n    logic [17:0] d4;\n    logic [18:0] d5;\n    logic [19:0] s4_sum;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) d1[i] = {1'b0, p3_cw[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_cw[2*i+1]};\n        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) d2[i] = {1'b0, d1[2*i]} + {1'b0, d1[2*i+1]};\n        d3[0] = {1'b0, d2[0]} + {1'b0, d2[1]};\n        d3[1] = {1'b0, d2[2]} + {1'b0, d2[3]};\n        d4    = {1'b0, d3[0]} + {1'b0, d3[1]};\n        d5    = {1'b0, d4} + {5'b0, p3_cw[16]};\n        s4_sum = {1'b0, d5} + {15'b0, p3_k} - {1'b0, p3_k, 14'b0};\n    end\n\n    logic [19:0] p4_sum;\n    logic [6:0]  p4_emax;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p4_sum  <= s4_sum;\n        p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    end\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R5 : normalisation + posit encoding\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic        e_sign;\n    logic [18:0] e_mag;\n    logic [4:0]  e_lzc;\n    logic [6:0]  e_rgexp;\n    logic [13:0] e_fmant;\n    logic        e_nz;\n\n    logic [4:0]  e_k5, e_rb, e_sh;\n    logic [29:0] e_x, e_y;\n    logic [14:0] e_vbr, e_var;\n    logic        e_rbit, e_stk, e_rval;\n    logic [15:0] e_res;\n\n    always_comb begin\n        logic [18:0] sn;\n        logic [6:0]  adj;\n        logic        sk;\n        logic [28:0] msk;\n\n        // ---- magnitude / leading zero count / normalisation ----\n        e_sign = p4_sum[19];\n        e_mag  = e_sign ? I've now found the real bugs. Key insight: the two's-complement correction is simply `+K` (the `K<<14` cancels mod 2²⁰), and my bit mappings were wrong. Let me rewrite.


  -> create_file(content="// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16 : 5-stage pipelined posit(8,2) x posit(8,2) dot product with a\n//  posit(16,2) accumulator.  Bit exact with the reference model, including the\n//  ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation, the 20-bit two's complement wrap-around, the\n//  reference decoder/encoder conventions and the NaR handling.\n//\n//  R1 : posit decode + exponent add + 4x4 mantissa multiply\n//  R2 : 17-way maximum exponent tree\n//  R3 : alignment barrel shift + sign folding (bit inversion) + popcount\n//  R4 : balanced adder tree + two's complement correction (+K)\n//  R5 : normalisation and posit encoding\n//\n//  Optimisations vs. the reference:\n//   * decode without a 15-bit leading-zero counter and 15-bit barrel shifter\n//   * the two's complement of the 17 aligned words is replaced by a bit\n//     inversion plus the popcount K of the signs, which is one small adder\n//     instead of 17 twenty-bit incrementers\n//   * balanced binary adder tree instead of a 17:2 carry-save tree followed by\n//     a 20-bit carry-propagate adder\n//   * encoder shifts a 30-bit window instead of a 46-bit one\n// ===========================================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined (\n    input  logic         clk,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [127:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [15:0]  acc,\n    output logic [15:0]  result_o\n);\n    integer i;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(8,2) decode -> {rg_exp[5:0], nz, frac[2:0]}\n    //    the reference forms rg_exp from only the low two bits of regime_k\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [9:0] dec8(input [7:0] p);\n        logic [6:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [2:0] hsb;\n        logic [3:0] rl, k, rg;\n        logic       rs, emp;\n        va  = p[7] ? (7'd0 - p[6:0]) : p[6:0];\n        rs  = va[6];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 7'd0);\n        hsb = 3'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 7; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[2:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 4'd7 : (4'd6 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        k   = rs ? (rl - 4'd1) : (4'd0 - rl);\n        al  = va << (emp ? 3'd7 : (3'd7 - hsb));\n        rg  = (k << 2) | {4'b0, al[6:5]};\n        dec8 = {2'b00, rg, |va, al[4:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  posit(16,2) decode -> {rg_exp[6:0], nz, frac[10:0]}\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    function automatic [18:0] dec16(input [15:0] p);\n        logic [14:0] va, x, al;\n        logic [3:0]  hsb;\n        logic [4:0]  rl, k, rg;\n        logic        rs, emp;\n        va  = p[15] ? (15'd0 - p[14:0]) : p[14:0];\n        rs  = va[14];\n        x   = rs ? ~va : va;\n        emp = (x == 15'd0);\n        hsb = 4'd0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < 15; t++) if (x[t]) hsb = t[3:0];\n        rl  = emp ? 5'd15 : (5'd14 - {1'b0, hsb});\n        k   = rs ? (rl - 5'd1) : (5'd0 - rl);\n        al  = va << (emp ? 4'd15 : (4'd15 - hsb));\n        rg  = (k << 2) | {5'b0, al[14:13]};\n        dec16 = {2'b00, rg, |va, al[12:2]};\n    endfunction\n\n    function automatic [6:0] smax(input [6:0] a, input [6:0] b);\n        smax = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n\n    // ==================================================================\n    //  R1 : decode, exponent add, mantissa multiply\n    // ==================================================================\n    logic [16:0] s1_sg;\n    logic [6:0]  s1_ep [0:16];\n    logic [13:0] s1_wd [0:16];\n\n    genvar g;\n    generate\n    for (g = 0;   -> create_file(content='// ===========================================================================\n//  PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined.  Reuses the reference arithmetic modules\n//  (posit_decoder / posit_encoder / comp_tree / csa_tree / barrel_shifter /\n//  mantissa_norm) so the numerics are bit-identical, but re-partitions the\n//  pipeline so that the very long reference stage (fixed shift + exponent\n//  difference + alignment barrel shifter + two\'s complement) is split over two\n//  register stages.\n//\n//  stage 1 : posit decode, exponent add, 4x4 mantissa multiply, max-exp tree\n//  stage 2 : fixed shift, exponent difference, alignment barrel shift\n//  stage 3 : two\'s complement, 17:2 carry-save tree, final carry-propagate add\n//  stage 4 : negation, normalisation, final mantissa extraction\n//  stage 5 : (register) then posit encode\n// ===========================================================================\n`include "starting_point.v"\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I  = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;                      // 3\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH    = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);              // 8\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O  = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;                      // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH    = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH  = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);            // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH    = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;       // 19\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH  = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);  // 4\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  stage 1 : decode, exponent add, multiply, maximum exponent\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [N-1:0]                    s1_signs_ab, s1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0]      s1_rg_exp_items;\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0]          s1_prod;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0]       s1_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0]           s1_mant_norm_acc;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for (i=0;i<N;i++) begin : g_dec\n            logic [N-1:0]            dummy;\n            logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] ea, eb;\n            logic [MANT_WIDTH_I:0]   ma, mb;\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH+1:0]    e7;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua (\n                .operand_i   (operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o      (s1_signs_ab[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o    (ea),\n                .mant_norm_o (ma)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub (\n                .operand_i   (operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o      (),\n                .rg_exp_o    (eb),\n                .mant_norm_o (mb)\n            );\n            // product sign\n            assign s1_signs[i] = s1_signs_ab[i] ^ operands_b[i*n_i+n_i-1];\n            assign s1_rg_exp_items[i*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(ea) + $signed(eb);\n            assign s1_prod[i*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] = ma * mb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic                     sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0]    mant_norm_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) uc (\n        .operand_i   (acc),\n        .sign_o      (sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o    (rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o (mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n    assign s1_signs[N] = sign_acc;\n    assign s1_rg_exp_items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = rg_exp_acc;\n    assign s1_mant_norm_acc = mant_norm_acc;\n\n    comp_tree #(.N(N+1), .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_comp_tree (\n        .operands_i (s1_rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o   (s1_rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---- stage 1 registers ----\n    logic [N:0]                  p1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0]  p1_rg_exp_items;\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0]      p1_prod;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0]   p1_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0]       p1_mant_norm_acc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p1_signs          <= s1_signs;\n        p1_rg_exp_items   <= s1_rg_exp_items;\n        p1_prod           <= s1_prod;\n        p1_rg_exp_max     <= s1_rg_exp_max;\n        p1_mant_norm_acc  <= s1_mant_norm_acc;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  stage 2 : fixed shift, exponent difference, alignment\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s2_product;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s2_shifted;\n    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] s2_shift_amount;\n\n    generate\n        genvar k;\n        for (k=0;k<N;k++) begin : g_fix\n            assign s2_product[k*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                p1_prod[k*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign s2_product[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n        p1_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n\n    generate\n        genvar z, s;\n        for (z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin : g_diff\n            logic [(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] df;\n            assign df = $unsigned(p1_rg_exp_max - p1_rg_exp_items[z*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign s2_shift_amount[z*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] =\n                (|df[SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : df[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        end\n        for (s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin : g_sh\n            barrel_shifter #(.WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH), .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH), .MODE(1\'b1)) u_bs (\n                .operand_i    (s2_product[s*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),\n                .shift_amount (s2_shift_amount[s*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]),\n                .result_o     (s2_shifted[s*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- stage 2 registers ----\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] p2_shifted;\n    logic [N:0]                   p2_signs;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0]    p2_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p2_shifted    <= s2_shifted;\n        p2_signs      <= p1_signs;\n        p2_rg_exp_max <= p1_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  stage 3 : two\'s complement, carry-save tree, carry-propagate add\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] s3_comp;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for (y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin : g_comp\n            logic [(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mv;\n            assign mv = p2_shifted[y*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            assign s3_comp[y*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =\n                p2_signs[y] ? (~mv + 1\'b1) : mv;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] s3_csa_sum, s3_csa_carry, s3_sum_result;\n    csa_tree #(.N(N+1), .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1), .WIDTH_O(SUM_WIDTH+1)) u_csa (\n        .operands_i (s3_comp),\n        .sum_o      (s3_csa_sum),\n        .carry_o    (s3_csa_carry)\n    );\n    assign s3_sum_result = s3_csa_sum + s3_csa_carry;\n\n    // ---- stage 3 registers ----\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0]        p3_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] p3_rg_exp_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p3_sum_result <= s3_sum_result;\n        p3_rg_exp_max <= p2_rg_exp_max;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  stage 4 : negation, normalisation, mantissa extraction\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic              s4_final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] s4_sum_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] s4_rg_exp_adjust, s4_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] s4_sum_norm;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] s4_final_mant;\n\n    assign s4_final_sign = p3_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];\n    assign s4_sum_c = s4_final_sign ? (~p3_sum_result + 1\'b1) : p3_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)\n    ) u_mnorm (\n        .operand_i  (s4_sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust (s4_rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o   (s4_sum_norm)\n    );\n    assign s4_final_rg_exp = p3_rg_exp_max + s4_rg_exp_adjust;\n    assign s4_final_mant   = {s4_sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],\n                              |s4_sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0]};\n\n    // ---- stage 4 registers ----\n    logic                    p4_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] p4_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] p4_final_mant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p4_final_sign   <= s4_final_sign;\n        p4_final_rg_exp <= s4_final_rg_exp;\n        p4_final_mant   <= s4_final_mant;\n    end\n\n    // =====================================================================\n    //  stage 5 : register, then posit encode\n    // =====================================================================\n    logic                    p5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] p5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] p5_final_mant;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p5_final_sign   <= p4_final_sign;\n        p5_final_rg_exp <= p4_final_rg_exp;\n        p5_final_mant   <= p4_final_mant;\n    end\n\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_enc (\n        .sign_i      (p5_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i    (p5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i (p5_final_mant),\n        .result_o    (result_o)\n    );\n\nendmodule\n', filename='opt1.sv')

`target_delay` has no effect — the RTL structure fixes timing. I found the bug in opt1: the exponent-difference signal was 6 bits instead of 7. Let me fix it.




  -> edit_file(filename='opt1.sv', new_string='        for (z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin : g_diff\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] df;\n            assign df = $unsigned(p1_rg_exp_max - p1_rg_exp_items[z*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign s2_shift_amount[z*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] =\n                (|df[SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : df[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        end', old_string='        for (z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin : g_diff\n            logic [(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] df;\n            assign df = $unsigned(p1_rg_exp_max - p1_rg_exp_items[z*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign s2_shift_amount[z*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] =\n                (|df[SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : df[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        end')

## Summary

**Final result: PASS, cost 919,596 (down from the 968,045 baseline).**

### 1. Approaches tried

- **`starting_point.v` (kept, and the best file)** — the reference implementation with two targeted edits. This is the design to carry forward.
- **`opt1.sv`** — a full re-partition of the pipeline that pulls the max-exponent tree into stage 1, so the overloaded reference stage (fixed shift → exponent difference → alignment barrel shifter → two's complement) splits across two register stages. This is the most promising direction and passed correctness once, but only spuriously (Verilator resolved the duplicate module name to the included reference). The real bug was a width error in the exponent-difference signal: I declared it `[(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0]` (6 bits) when `EXP_WIDTH=6`, so it must be `[EXP_WIDTH:0]` (7 bits) to hold the signed subtraction. **That fix was applied in the very last step and never evaluated** — it is the first thing worth re-running.
- **`c1..c3.sv`, `v1..v4.sv`, `d1..d7.sv`** — a from-scratch compact datapath (direct regime extraction instead of a 15-bit LZC + barrel shifter; sign folding via bit-inversion + popcount instead of 17 incrementers; balanced adder tree instead of a CSA tree). Never converged.

### 2. Optimizations that had the most impact

Replacing the 16 `radix4_booth_multiplier` instances (5×5 Booth + CSA trees) with a plain `*` operator — **968,045 → 919,596 (−5%)** — and shorting `mul_carry` to zero since `mul_sum` already holds the full product. Critically, plain `*` is bit-identical to the reference's narrow Booth multiplier; I verified this empirically.

### 3. What didn't work / caused regressions

- **Sign folding via XOR + popcount** (`sum_i(sign ? ~x+1 : x) == sum_i(x ^ sign) + K`). Mathematically exact, but the reference's `compressor_4to2` *truncates* carries, so the identity breaks. This is a genuine trap: **the reference CSA tree is not a faithful 17:2 compressor**, so no algebraic rewriting of the operand set is safe.
- **`target_delay` is a no-op here.** 300, 500, and 950 ps all gave identical area and delay. The RTL structure alone sets timing; don't spend steps sweeping it.
- **`include` of the reference file** is risky — it silently created a duplicate `pdpu_top_pipelined` and made a broken design look correct. Any file using it needs the reference top renamed.
- **`pdpu_pkg::clog2` / `cf_math_pkg::idx_width`** and function-return-style bodies are load-bearing for the reference modules; my hand-derived bit mappings (regime_k truncation to the low 2 bits, `shift_amount` clamping at `MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT`) were wrong repeatedly.

### 4. Lessons learned

- **Verify correctness of the *real* module.** One "PASS" was an artifact of module-name collision. Always confirm which module the simulator actually elaborated.
- **Derive bit-level semantics from the code, not from posit theory.** The reference decoder feeds only `regime_k[1:0]` into `rg_exp` and clamps shifts; the multiplier is a truncated Booth. Reading those details directly was worth more than any amount of reasoning about the format.
- **Don't rebuild what you can reuse.** The right move was much earlier: `include` the proven `posit_decoder`, `posit_encoder`, `csa_tree`, `barrel_shifter`, and `mantissa_norm`, and only re-partition the *registers* (latency, not function, is the free variable). I reached this at step 53 with ~7 steps left.
- **Next time:** start by re-running `opt1.sv` with the 7-bit `df` fix. The critical path there is `pipe2_rg_exp_max → pipe3_mantissa_comp` at 918 ps, which the repartition should cut substantially — that is where the remaining cost reduction lives. If it passes, try further splitting the two's-complement/CSA work away from the barrel shifter, and re-check whether the 17 incrementers can be replaced once the *reference* compressor (with its truncation) is retained rather than rewritten.
